# V8 complete per-seed tables

The separately written native/data/optimizer/statistics checker and the proxy-gradient audit passed. The tables are generated from the saved one-shot test output; no scientific training or held-out evaluation was repeated for reporting. These checks are internal to the workflow, not external independent replication.

All 416 trained models are retained on both final training and held-out data. The four fixed POINT configurations share inputs and initialization within each of 104 paired training/data blocks. Each block has 128 training episodes and 512 fresh held-out episodes. Accuracy and class recall count abstention as error. The inferential unit is 104 paired blocks, not 416 independent models or individual episodes.

Raw full precision, all signed-count-margin histograms and complete final native arrays are retained in results/TRAIN_LOCK.json, results/TEST_SUMMARY.json and the per-model archives. This table is a readable projection of those saved records.

## Single primary interaction

The primary paired accuracy interaction is (CE/.03−CE/.01)−(RATE/.03−RATE/.01). Its two-sided Student-t95 interval governs interpretation. The percentile bootstrap95 interval is a prespecified sensitivity analysis, not an alternate success criterion. All displayed differences are percentage points.

| n blocks | Estimate (pp) | Sample SD (pp) | SE (pp) | df | t critical | Primary t95 interval (pp) | Realized half-width (pp) | Bootstrap95 sensitivity (pp) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 104 | -14.1057 | 43.697729 | 4.284915 | 103 | 1.983264144773 | [-22.6038, -5.6076] | 8.498118 | [-22.5625, -5.8424] |
